# Debug Toolpack 11.3.8 — сгенерировано tools/gen3.py. Правьте генератор, а не этот файл.
dtp_sys_marked = {
	has_star_flag = dtp_marked
}

dtp_has_perm = {
	OR = {
		has_country_flag = dtp_host
		AND = {
			is_ai = no
			has_country_flag = dtp_perm_$P$
		}
	}
}

dtp_has_any_perm = {
	OR = {
		dtp_has_perm = { P = res }
		dtp_has_perm = { P = planet }
		dtp_has_perm = { P = state }
		dtp_has_perm = { P = species }
		dtp_has_perm = { P = diplo }
		dtp_has_perm = { P = fleet }
		dtp_has_perm = { P = event }
		dtp_has_perm = { P = force }
		dtp_has_perm = { P = gm }
	}
}

dtp_is_host = {
	has_country_flag = dtp_host
}

dtp_force_active = {
	has_country_flag = dtp_force_mode
	dtp_has_perm = { P = force }
}

dtp_military_fleet = {
	any_owned_ship = {
		is_ship_class = shipclass_military
	}
}

dtp_target_country = {
	OR = {
		dtp_listed_country = yes
		is_primitive = yes
	}
}

dtp_listed_country = {
	OR = {
		is_country_type = default
		is_country_type = fallen_empire
		is_country_type = awakened_fallen_empire
		is_country_type = dtp_gamemaster
	}
}

dtp_tr_differs = {
	OR = {
		AND = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 1 }
			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_rcp_1
			exists = event_target:dtp_tc_1
			event_target:dtp_tc_1 = {
				NOT = { is_same_value = event_target:dtp_rcp_1 }
			}
		}
		AND = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 2 }
			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_rcp_2
			exists = event_target:dtp_tc_2
			event_target:dtp_tc_2 = {
				NOT = { is_same_value = event_target:dtp_rcp_2 }
			}
		}
		AND = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 3 }
			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_rcp_3
			exists = event_target:dtp_tc_3
			event_target:dtp_tc_3 = {
				NOT = { is_same_value = event_target:dtp_rcp_3 }
			}
		}
		AND = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 4 }
			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_rcp_4
			exists = event_target:dtp_tc_4

				NOT = { is_same_value = event_target:dtp_rcp_4 }

		AND = {

		AND = {

			exists = event_target:dtp_tc_7

			exists = event_target:dtp_tc_9

dtp_spx = {
	OR = {
		AND = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 1 }
			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_tc_1
			OR = {
				AND = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_tsp_1
					event_target:dtp_tsp_1 = {
						$T$ = yes
					}
				}
				AND = {
					NOT = { exists = event_target:dtp_tsp_1 }
					event_target:dtp_tc_1 = {
						owner_species = {
							$T$ = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		AND = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 2 }

						$T$ = yes
					}
				}
				AND = {
					NOT = { exists = event_target:dtp_tsp_7 }
					event_target:dtp_tc_7 = {
						owner_species = {
							$T$ = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		AND = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 8 }
			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_tc_8
			OR = {
				AND = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_tsp_8
					event_target:dtp_tsp_8 = {
						$T$ = yes
					}
				}
				AND = {
					NOT = { exists = event_target:dtp_tsp_8 }
					event_target:dtp_tc_8 = {
						owner_species = {
							$T$ = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		AND = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 9 }
			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_tc_9
			OR = {
				AND = {

					event_target:dtp_tsp_9 = {
						$T$ = yes

dtp_ok_eth_militarist = {
	NOT = { has_ethic = ethic_gestalt_consciousness }
	NOR = { has_ethic = ethic_pacifist has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_pacifist }
}

dtp_ok_ethp_militarist = {
	NOT = { calc_true_if = {
			amount >= 3
			has_ethic = ethic_xenophobe
			has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe
			AND = {
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe
			}
			has_ethic = ethic_xenophile
			has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophile
			AND = {
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophile
			}
			has_ethic = ethic_authoritarian
			has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_authoritarian
			AND = {
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_authoritarian
			}
			has_ethic = ethic_egalitarian
			has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian
			AND = {
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian
			}
			has_ethic = ethic_spiritualist
			has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_spiritualist
			AND = {
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_spiritualist
			}
			has_ethic = ethic_materialist
			has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_materialist
			AND = {
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_materialist
			}
		} }
}

dtp_ok_eth_f_militarist = {
	NOT = { has_ethic = ethic_gestalt_consciousness }
	NOR = { has_ethic = ethic_pacifist has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_pacifist }
}

	NOT = { calc_true_if = {
			amount >= 2
			has_ethic = ethic_xenophobe
			has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe
			AND = {
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe

			has_ethic = ethic_egalitarian
			has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian
			AND = {
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian
			}
			has_ethic = ethic_spiritualist
			has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_spiritualist
			AND = {
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_spiritualist
			}
			has_ethic = ethic_materialist
			has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_materialist
			AND = {
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_materialist
			}
		} }

dtp_esl_sel = {
	OR = {
		AND = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 1 }
			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_tsp_1
			exists = event_target:dtp_esl_$J$
			event_target:dtp_tsp_1 = {
				is_same_species = event_target:dtp_esl_$J$
			}
		}
		AND = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 2 }
			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_tsp_2
			exists = event_target:dtp_esl_$J$

dtp_tsp_none = {
	OR = {
		AND = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 1 }
			}
			NOT = { exists = event_target:dtp_tsp_1 }
		}
		AND = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 2 }
			}
			NOT = { exists = event_target:dtp_tsp_2 }
		}
		AND = {

		is_archetype = ROBOT

		species_portrait = cyb_machine
		species_portrait = default_robot
		species_portrait = sd_hum_robot

		species_portrait = sd_avi_robot

dtp_tpx = {
	OR = {
		AND = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 1 }
			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_tp_1
			event_target:dtp_tp_1 = {
				$T$ = yes
			}
		}
		AND = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 2 }
			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_tp_2
			event_target:dtp_tp_2 = {
				$T$ = yes
			}
		}
		AND = {

			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_tp_3
